Like, I’ll go a day barely eating or forgetting meals, and then some nights after midnight I’m just going through takeout and snacks. That back and forth is honestly part of what scared me into coming in.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're in control when you're eating late at night and having a lot of food?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. It feels like once I start, especially late after midnight, I just keep grabbing more takeout or snacks. It’s not every night, but the way I can forget meals some days and then eat like that other nights is what scared me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're unable to stop yourself from eating when you have these late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it feels that way sometimes. What brings you in to see me today?\n\nPatient: Honestly, what got me to come in is my eating has been really all over the place. Some days I just forget to eat meals completely, and then other nights I’m up after midnight tearing through takeout and snacks. It kind of scared me how uneven it’s been.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your weight recently?\n\nPatient: I haven’t really tracked the number closely, but my appetite’s been changing a lot. Some days I’ll barely eat or forget meals, and then other nights I’m up late eating a bunch of takeout or snacks. It’s been uneven enough that it worried me.\n\nDoctor: Do you worry about gaining weight when you think about your eating habits?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. I’m not sitting there worried about gaining weight. It’s more that the pattern feels off—like skipping meals and then eating a lot later.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel guilty after eating?\n\nPatient: Not really. I mean, maybe I feel annoyed with myself sometimes because it’s not a normal routine, but I wouldn’t say I feel guilty after eating.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you can't stop eating once you've started?\n\nPatient: Yeah, sometimes. It’s not every time I eat, but there are nights where once I start with takeout or snacks, especially late after midnight, I just keep going. It feels like my eating’s been all over the place—some days I forget meals completely, then other nights I’m tearing through food.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually eat more when you're feeling stressed or emotional?\n\nPatient: Yeah, kind of. I don’t know if it’s exactly stress or emotions every time, but when things feel off I’ll either forget to eat all day or then later, usually after midnight, I’m just tearing through takeout and snacks. That’s honestly one of the things that made me think something wasn’t right.\n\nDoctor: How often do you have nights where you tear through food like that?\n\nPatient: It’s not every night, but often enough that it bothered me. Like, I’ll go a day barely eating or forgetting meals, and then some nights after midnight I’m just going through takeout and snacks. That back and forth is honestly part of what scared me into coming in.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're in control when you're eating late at night and having a lot of food?\n\nPatient: Not really, no. It feels like once I start, especially late after midnight, I just keep grabbing more takeout or snacks. It’s not every night, but the way I can forget meals some days and then eat like that other nights is what scared me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're unable to stop yourself from eating when you have these late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah, it feels that way sometimes. Like once I start eating that late, I’m not really thinking “okay, stop now.” I’ll just keep going through whatever takeout or snacks I have. It’s not every night. Some days I’ll forget meals completely, and then on other nights—usually after midnight—I’m tearing through takeout and snacks. That back and forth is what really scared me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're eating much more quickly than usual during those late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah, sometimes. I don’t know if I’m timing it, but it feels like I’m just tearing through the food, especially after midnight with takeout or snacks. Then other days I’ll realize I barely ate at all, so it’s been really all over the place.\n\nDoctor: Do you have these episodes where you feel like you can't stop eating more than three times a week?\n\nPatient: I don’t think it’s more than three times a week. It’s more like some nights after midnight I’ll tear through takeout or snacks, but then other days I forget meals completely. That back and forth is what’s been worrying me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you have no control over what you're eating during those late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah, in those moments it feels pretty out of control. Some days I’ll barely eat or forget meals, and then other nights I’m up late eating a bunch of takeout or snacks. I’m not sitting there worried about gaining weight. I mean, maybe I feel annoyed with myself sometimes because it’s not a normal routine, but I wouldn’t say I feel guilty after eating.\n\nDoctor: Do you ever feel like you can't stop eating once you've started?\n\nPatient: Yeah, sometimes. It’s not every time I eat, but there are nights where once I start with takeout or snacks, especially late after midnight, I just keep going. It feels like my eating’s been all over the place—some days I forget meals completely, then other nights I’m tearing through food.\n\nDoctor: Do you usually eat more when you're feeling stressed or emotional?\n\nPatient: Yeah, kind of. It’s not every time I eat, but there are nights where once I start with takeout or snacks, especially late after midnight, I just keep going. It feels like once I start, especially late after midnight, I just keep grabbing more takeout or snacks. Like once I start eating that late, I’m not really thinking “okay, stop now.” I’ll just keep going through whatever takeout or snacks I have. It’s not every night. Some days I’ll forget meals completely, and then on other nights—usually after midnight—I’m tearing through takeout and snacks. That back and forth is what really scared me.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel like you're eating much more quickly than usual during those late-night episodes?\n\nPatient: Yeah, sometimes. I don’t know if I’m timing it, but it feels like I’m just tearing through the food, especially after midnight with takeout or snacks. Then other days I’ll realize I barely ate at all, so it’s been really all over the place.\n\nDoctor: Do you have these episodes where you feel like you can't stop eating more than three times a week?\n\nPatient: I don’t think it’s more than three times a week.
